## Bots and the AI subsystem (2026-09-21; pathfinding and the follower AI-26..AI-32, then sensing, the contest, Fire / Scout / TakeCover, the strategic AI and spawning AI-33..AI-38, all read and built 2026-09-23; vehicles and the medic stay open)

The engine's AI, read for
[`features/bf1942-ai-research-2026-09-21/`](../bf1942-ai-research-2026-09-21/README.md).
All addresses are `bf1942_lnxded.static` unless a row says *client*. The patched
copy read has a symbol table byte-identical to the corpus's and differs in 90
bytes, none in `dice::bf::ai` (AI-23). Every row below was re-derived by an
independent second reader, who **overturned AI-4** and corrected three more.

| # | Finding | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| AI-1 | A bot is an ordinary player: `BotMain::updatePlayerAction` `0x08526430` hands its `PlayerInput` to `AIPlayer::addInput` `0x085dcf10`, which packs a `PlayerAction` and pushes it into the **same `ActionBuffer` the network fills**. No bot-specific path exists below the input layer | **verified** | `0x085dcf10` is two calls, `PlayerAction::set` then `ActionBuffer::pushBack`; `BFPlayer+0x144` holds a *pointer to* the buffer, not the buffer inline. `GameServer::simulatePlayersUpdate` `0x0815bfa0` runs every player whose `getClient(id)` is 0 - the bot case |
| AI-2 | `PlayerInputMap` is 55 named channels registered one `addConstantHelper` call each; ch30 is `PICameraMode1`, and **there is no `PIJump`** - the engine's jump is `c_PIAction` = 9 | **verified** | `dice::ref2::io::Module::init()` `0x083f8870`, helper `0x083f7f40`, name strings from `0x086c86a5`; decoded twice by two independent scripts with identical output. Jump channel per [handweapon-view-and-deviation.md](subsystems/handweapon-view-and-deviation.md) §2 |
| AI-3 | `PlayerInput` is **55 floats at offset 0**, indexed by `PlayerInputMap`, with a 55-bit mapped-mask at `+0xdc`/`+0xe0` | **verified** | `EntryInfanteryResetControls::execute` `0x08617c40` writes `*(u32*)(pi + index*4) = 0` behind a bounds check that errors above `0x36`; `Input<PlayerInputMap,55u>::isInputMapped` `0x08056210` bound-checks `> 0x36` and shifts the `+0xdc`/`+0xe0` pair. `55 * 4 = 0xdc`, so the mask starts immediately after the array |
| AI-4 | ~~The AI runs **last** in the simulation tick, so a bot pays one tick (1/30 s) of input latency~~ | **refuted** (2026-09-21, second reader) | `updateAI` is at `+0x1b3` **by layout, not by execution**. At `0x0815c2b8` a `dec eax / je 0x0815c449` on `Game::getGameStatus` (`0x080617b0`) jumps to a cold block gcc parked at the end of the function; that block calls `updateAI` and then `jmp 0x0815c2be` at `0x0815c45b`, re-entering the body one instruction past the branch, **before** `simulatePlayersUpdate` at `0x0815c2c8`. **The AI runs first**, gated on `GameStatus == 1` (Playing, from `updateGameLogic` `0x081505c0`). A bot's action is therefore in the buffer before the consumer runs; the realised latency stays **open** because `simulatePlayerUpdate` `0x0815bd00` selects by a sequence index that was not decoded |
| AI-5 | `Game::updateAI` `0x0805da50` / `GameServer::updateAI` `0x0813a3f0` call `IAIMain::instance` (`0x0874fb8c`) slots `+0x1c` `isInitialised`, `+0x24` `tick(dt)`, `+0x28` `action()` through the secondary vptr at `vtable for AIMain` **+0x80** | **verified**, offset corrected from +0x78 | `+0x78` holds the secondary offset-to-top `0xfffffffc` and `+0x7c` the typeinfo; the thunks are at symbol `+0x9c`/`+0xa4`/`+0xa8`. Exactly the trap this file's own preamble warns about - the gcc "+8" rule applied to the primary table and forgotten for the secondary |
| AI-6 | The AI is **time-budgeted, not per-bot-per-tick**: `AIMain::action` takes `getTimeIncrease() * aiSystemQuotient` and `BotManager::action` splits `0.95x` of it into 0.40 plan-execution, `0.60*q_dec` decision, `0.60*q_path` pathfinding, `0.60*(1-q_dec-q_path)*{0.1, 0.9}` hearing/sensing | **verified** | `AIMain::action` `0x084778d0` (quotient `AIMain+0x10`, `setAISystemQuotient` `0x08477840`; components `setAISystemComponentsQuotient` `0x08477880` -> `+0x18`/`+0x20`); `BotManager::action(double const&)` `0x0849a130`; `setSystemQuotient(a,b,c)` `0x08498cd0` stores `a/(a+b+c)` and `b/(a+b+c)`. Gazala's `40 40 20` gives 0.40 / 0.24 / 0.24 / 0.012 / 0.108 |
| AI-7 | Bots are topped up **per tick** to `aiSettings.setMaxNBots`, each created as a real player and handed to `AIMain::activateBot` | **verified**; the two gating bytes are **inferred** | `GameServer::gameStatusPlaying` `0x08150df0` at `0x08150e8b`: `while (getMaxNBots() != getBotCount())` -> create player -> `IAIMain` vptr +0x10 = `AIMain::activateBot` `0x08478510` -> `addBot` `0x08477f30` -> `BotManager::addBot` `0x08499060`. The gates at `GameServer+0x64` and `+0x468` are inferred to be `game.isAiLevel` / `game.autoSpawnBots`, which reach GameServer through virtual setters |
| AI-8 | Bot names load per game mode: mode 2 runs `<level>SinglePlayer/Bots.con`, every other mode `SinglePlayer/Skirmish.con`; both only `run` the `Bf1942/Game/common/*Names.con` lists | **verified** | `GameServer::loadBots(int)` `0x08131ef0`, called from `startServer` at `0x08133582`; path literals `0x086bff2d` and `0x086bff13` |
| AI-9 | `aiTemplate.addType ITxxx` sets `AITemplate+0x08 \|= 1 << enumValue`; `InformationType` is a dense 0..29 enum | **verified** | Console record `0x087cc3c0`, handler `ConsoleClass492::executeObjectMethod` `0x08500980`. Enum re-decoded independently from the jump table `0x086f3f5c` of `operator<<(ostream&, InformationType)` `0x08485290`; `ITAir = 4`, `ITNaval = 6`, `ITSoldier = 22` |
| AI-10 | `aiMeshes` bind **by name, not by reference**: the geometry template name truncated at the **last** `_`, plus `"_a1"`, under `"AIMeshes/"`, loaded as an ordinary StandardMesh | **verified** | `AIMeshLoader::getAIMesh(IGeometry*)` `0x08479940` (`rfind(0x5f)` at `0x0847999b`, rodata `0x086f315c` = `_a1`) -> `getAIMesh(string const&)` `0x08479a10` (rodata `0x086f3160` = `AIMeshes/`). Closes the round README's "`aiMeshes.rfa` hulls" open item |
| AI-11 | Vanilla's `aiMeshes.rfa` is 180 entries (90 `.sm` + 90 `.rs`); all 90 parse, versions 9 (83) / 10 (7), and all **140 material descriptors are vertex-flags `0x411`, primitive 4** | **confirmed** (data) | Re-parsed with `bf42.stdmesh`; the `.rs` carries `materialDiffuse 0 0.7 0.3`. `AIMeshVertex::vertexFormat` `0x0874f2f8` = `0x11` belongs to the loader, **not** to the file |
| AI-12 | The shipped navigation maps live in **`Pathfinding/`**, not `AIPathfinding/`, and are **loaded, not generated**: 19 of vanilla's 23 levels ship them, and GCMOD, Pirates and interstate ship none | **verified** (loader) / **confirmed** (survey) | `AIPathfinding::loadMaps` `0x0847c580` -> `loadSearchMaps` `0x0847c5c0` builds `levelPath + "Pathfinding/"` (rodata `0x086f3198`) -> `LocalMap::loadRawFile` `0x085fefb0`. `createMaps` `0x0847b2e0` / `floodLevelZeroMap` `0x085fbae0` is the editor path. | AI-13 | A `Pathfinding/*.raw` opens with five int32 the loader refuses the file over - `p6-p5`, `p7-p5`, `p5`, `p3`, `p4` from the `CellMap` ctor - then a special-cell count and `(1<<(p6-p5))<<(p7-p5)` row records; `p5 = 6 + level` in every sample | **open** (header verified, row encoding unread) | `CellMap::loadRawFile` `0x085f86a0` validates against `CellMap+0x18/0x20/0x10/0x2c/0x24`; ctor `0x085f7af0`. Six observed headers fit exactly |
| AI-14 | Difficulty has **exactly one engine-side knob**, `aiSettings.setBotSkill`, and vanilla never sets it - the running value is `AISettings::reset()`'s default **0.75**, the game's own HARD | **verified** | `AISettings::reset()` `0x08484450` writes `+0x24 = 0x3f400000` at `0x08484613`; `getBotSkill` `0x08484760` / `setBotSkill` `0x08484770` are bare load/store, unclamped, `objectHasRange()` returns 0. Archive survey over vanilla + 18 mods: FH, FHSW, EoD, bfheroes and WarFront set 1.0, bg42 0.8, WarFront also 0.7; vanilla, XPack1/2, DC_Final, DesertCombat, GCMOD, Pirates, FinnWars and interstate never call it |
| AI-15 | `getBotSkill` has **exactly ten call sites in the whole server** - plan-step rate and trigger accuracy. It does not touch health, damage, speed, spotting or reaction | **verified by two independent enumerations** | `actionExecutePlan` `0x0849a41f` (`step = (skill*0.1 + 0.1) * getTimeIncrease()`), `ConsoleClass177` (the getter), `EntryInfoWrapper::execute` `0x08618380`, `EntryTrigger::execute` x4, `EntryTriggerContinously::execute` x3. The second reader re-did it without the first's 15-instruction heuristic: all **578** `call [reg+0x40]` sites in `.text` triaged by FPU consumption and receiver, zero direct calls to `0x08484760`, and the singleton `0x0874fe8c` never escapes into a field or a direct-call argument |
| AI-16 | Bot aim error: `dev = (1 - 0.75A)*C + F30*(F38*max(0, F34*(1-A) - (now-B)) + 0.25*(1-A))`, A = botSkill, B = `getFiringTargetTime()`, C in {0, 10, 30} | **verified** | `WeaponFireArm::setBotSkill` `0x085ee580`, x87 `0x085ee587`-`0x085ee60b` re-derived instruction by instruction by both readers; `fucom` / `test ah,0x45` / `je 0x085ee610` is the `D > 0` arm, so it is `max(0, D)` either way. Constants `[0x086ba8cc]` = 0.75, `[0x086c08ac]` = 0.25. Argument order re-derived from the push sequence `0x08625daf`-`0x08625dc9`, not assumed. Tail-jumps to `FireArms::setAIDeviation` `0x0828e350`. At the shipped 0.75: 1.5625 crosshair-radii at acquisition, 0.3125 steady, 2.5 s to settle; at 1.0 a perfect shot instantly |

Closes DEV-8's registration half |
| AI-18 | The `C` term is an **anti-aircraft penalty on the shooter**, not a penalty keyed on the target: 0 when the bot's own equipment is `ITAir` or its firing target is not `ITAir`, then 10 (naval, or an unnamed predicate) or 30 | **verified**, reading corrected | Two *different* `Information` records: `E` from `Bot` vptr `+0xd0` `getCurrentEquipment` at `0x08625cf0` and `T` from `+0x80` `getFiringTarget` at `0x08625dd7`, both resolved through the handle table `0x087beee0`; branches `0x08625d81` / `0x08625e18` / `0x08625e5c` / `0x08625e64`. The first reader had it as two tests on the target and cited `0x08625d91`, which is not an instruction boundary. Usefully, this confirms **`C = 0` for the infantry case** |
| AI-19 | `FireArms+0x190` (`aiPending`) is the bot term and nothing else - a one-shot additive contribution added into the deviation total and zeroed | **confirmed**; closes the corpus's unexplained `aiPending` channel | Writers: ctor `0x0828650f` (zero) and `setAIDeviation` `0x0828e350`/`0x0828e36e`. | AI-20 | *Client.* The Instant Battle difficulty variables convert as AiSkill 1/2/3/4 -> botSkill **0.25 / 0.5 / 0.75 / 1.0**; BotRatio -> 1:1 / 3:4 / 1:2 / 1:3; NrOfLives -> 1 / 5 / 10 / 20 | **verified** (tables); **inferred** that the ratio pair is the team ratios | `FUN_006dd910` `0x006dd910`, jump tables `0x006dda18` / `0x006dda28` / `0x006dda38`, all read from the PE by both readers. The float reaches `IAISettings::setBotSkill` as **arg 6** of `FUN_0044e7b0` (`[esp+0x42c]`, singleton `0x00a78610`, vtable `+0x40`, call at `0x0044e7f3`); the slot is confirmed by the client's own `aiSettings.setBotSkill` handler `0x006f3310` |
| AI-21 | *Client.* **The Instant Battle AI SKILL slider is inert in retail 1.61.** The menu writes `Skirmish/SkirmishAiSkill` to the int at `SkirmishSettings+0x64`; `FUN_006dd910`'s skirmish arm reads a **byte at `+0x88`** which has a getter and **no setter anywhere in the image**, so that path always yields 0.5 | **verified negative** (2026-09-21; was flagged open by the first reader, settled by the second) | Object unambiguous: `new 0x8c` at `0x006ddb74`, ctor `0x006dc800` zeroing `+0x88` at `0x006dc84b`, vptr `0x0092454c` occurring twice in the image. The brush for `1.0` is a **five-pixel plus**: `n = round(b) * 2 + 1`, bit where `(col + 0.5 - c)^2 + (row + 0.5 - c)^2 <= b^2` | **verified** (formula); **inferred** that `ITerrain +0x58` is a slope angle | `LocalMap::update` 0x085fe090, `LocalMapInfo::update` 0x08480f50, `MapBuffer::paintBrush` 0x086011b0, `CellMap::setBlob` 0x085f9250 |

Refreshes: `setTime(t)` sets +0x3c = t; `event_SpottedEnemyObject` 0x08523ae0 calls it on the spotted object's information and on every secondary's (vt+0x80 / vt+0x78, the other seats of the hull); `updateMemory` 0x085244e0 on a remembered object each time a re-test sees it (0x08524b05); `event_soundEmitter` 0x085237c0 and `updateHearingMemory` 0x085240e0 call `setTime(t, 0.5)` 0x085e8210, which clamps q to 0..1 and, only when the current security is below q, sets +0x1c = -1 and calls `setTime(t - 1 / (q x +0x40))` (`de f1` = `fdivrp`, 1 / (q D), checked against the bytes; q = 0 sets +0x3c = 0). `SCurve::calculate` 0x08658420: 1 from 1, 0 below 0, else the 101-entry `SCurve::init` 0x08658030 table blended at `trunc(100 x)` (the control word is set to truncate at 0x08658457; Ghidra shows ROUND). `SAI::updateStrengths` 0x08636c20 walks its own side's grid (env vt+0xdc) with a `UnitPredicate` and weighs each occupied unit by that information's vt+0x18. The loop: serial `BAPWrapperWeapon` (the magazine into the count), then parallel {`ResetControls(Reload)` when `getNMags` is not 1, the aim, the trigger}, then `If(not empty) { BAPANotifyBot(shot), counter += 1 }`, run while `counter < budget` (AI-131) and the target lives (`BAPConOpLessThan<int>`, `Not(BAPConObjectHealth)`), broken by `SharedCondition(empty)` only when `!hasAutoReload` (0x085ee890) and `getNMags` (0x085ef650) is not 1, and by `Not(BAPConWeaponHeat(0.8, 0.5))` when `hasHeat`. So an automatic weapon fires at its `roundOfFire` while the aim holds, and a Bazooka's plan does not end on its empty magazine but rides the 5.6 s reload out | **verified** (2026-09-24) | `viewer/bot-plans.js firePlanDone`, `viewer/bot-referee.js magazineTick`, features/bot-weapons |
| AI-131 | **A fire plan's round budget is 2 to 5 pulses for a single-shot weapon and 1 to 10 rounds for a burst weapon.** `BBPFireInfantery::createPlan` 0x085a3870, state 1 (fire from here), the firing state's +8 written at 0x085a51c1: `round(2 + 3u)` when the template's burst byte is 0, else `round(1 + u (n - 1))` with n the template's +0x10 held to [1, 10] (10 when below 1), u = `((Rand >> 7 \| 1) + 1) x 2^-24` in (0, 1]. The template's +0x10 is written once (`WeaponTemplate::init` 0x085efd40, its +2 latch) by `WeaponFireArm::init` 0x085ee220 with `getAmmo` (vt+0x28, `WeaponFireArmReal::getAmmo` 0x085ef630 = `FireArms::getTotalAmmo` 0x0828cb60: every magazine's rounds summed, -1 for unlimited), so n is 10 for every vanilla automatic weapon; +0xc takes `getAmmo x getNMags`. The move states write 0 or 1. AI-36's "up to 10 shots" for a single-shot weapon merged the two branches | **verified** (2026-09-24) | not ported (features/bot-weapons): the viewer keeps 1 to 10 pulses and no burst limit, `viewer/bot-fire.js firePlanFor`, `viewer/bot-plans.js planFire` |
| AI-132 | **`BBFire`'s weapon value divides by the rounds the weapon carries and reads the strength of the target's class; a bot's projectile striking an object counts a hit on that object's record, not on a weapon slot.** `BBFire::calculateUrgency` 0x08563570 (0x085638f0..): `strength[type] / ((20 (shots - hits) + 10) / ammo + 1)`, `ammo` the weapon's vt+0x28 (`getTotalAmmo`; -1 read as 0x10000, below 1 scores 0), `type` the target information's vt+0x40 indexing the template's strength table (+0x3c); shots and hits are the spotted record's per-slot words (+0x34, +0x54). `AICollisionHandler::handleCollision` 0x084640c0 adds one to +0x30 of the firing bot's record of the struck root (`BotMain::getSpottedObjectFromHandle` vt+0x8c, 0x08525b30) and hands the struck object's own bot a `FireObject` (`event_projectile` vt+0x148); `event_shotHit` 0x08526a40 only follows the watched round (+0x138). The writer of the per-slot hits (+0x54) was not found. The viewer now reads the target's class (it read Infantry for every target, so a German AT soldier's WalterP38, 3, beat his Panzershreck, 2, against a tank); it keeps ammo read as unlimited while rounds are left, a measured PARITY DEPARTURE (bot-referee.js `magazineTick`: the bots' ~5 % hit rate at 40 m sent them to their knives) | **verified** (the formula, the class), **open** (the per-slot hits writer) | `viewer/bot-perception.js chooseFiringTarget`, `viewer/bot-referee.js magazineTick` |
| AI-133 | **A reload starts only once the last round's fire cycle has run out, 1 / roundOfFire after it.** `FireArms::Fire` 0x0828a090 sets `timeToFireFinished` (0x0828fd60: +0x170 of the outermost FireArms) to the FireArms' +0x234, which its ctor writes as `1 / template+0x240` (0x08286870); +0x240 is `roundOfFire` (`FireArmsTemplate::makeScript` 0x0828e3b0 prints it as `ObjectTemplate.roundOfFire`; the template ctor 0x0828d4e0 starts it at 10). `FireArms::handleMessage` 0x082895c0 calls `Fire` for the fire messages (7, and 6 for the alternate) only while that timer is spent, and calls `Reload` for message 9 only while the reload, eject-clip and fire timers are all spent and the magazine is not full with another to change to (or no round is chambered). Every reload goes through 9: the player's, `handleUpdate` 0x08288890's for an autoReload template (+0x262, which the template ctor starts at 0) once the timers are spent and the magazine is empty, and `Fire`'s on an empty autoReload magazine. So a Bazooka (roundOfFire 1.0, reloadTime 5.6) fires once every 6.6 s, and a round's fire animation plays out before the reload's. The same read as BODY-7 (features/bot-body-animation) | **verified** (2026-09-24) | `viewer/bot-referee.js magazineTick`; the human's `hand-fire.js` still starts a change on the frame the magazine runs dry |

## Netcode (2026-09-20; the client send-batch cap, the ghost apply step, one ClientConnection ctor, and the register→wire send path stay open)

The engine's multiplayer architecture, read for
[`features/netcode-play-multiplayer/`](../netcode-play-multiplayer/README.md)
(P0 research; P2's wire format): an authoritative server stepping the same
fixed 30 Hz simulation the client runs, consuming one buffered `PlayerAction`
per tick per player, with the client predicting its own player and remote
entities ghost-overwritten at 0.1 s. Re-derived by an independent verifier
(verdict 2026-09-20, binding); narrative:
[subsystems/netcode.md](subsystems/netcode.md). All addresses
`bf1942_lnxded.static` unless marked client. The verifier's own caveats carry
through: NEEDS-MORE rows stay open, and nothing here was inferred from the
first reader's text.

| # | Finding | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| W-1 | The wire action record is **`PlayerAction`**: six 12-bit channels over bytes 0–11 in fixed order **Yaw, Pitch, Roll, Throttle, MouseLookX, MouseLookY**, then a **u32 button mask at byte 12** — 104 bits; byte 16 is never serialized | **verified** | `writeToStream` `0x08148750` / `readFromStream` `0x081486f0`: six `(write\|read)Unsigned(stream, 12)` into/from a `short[0..5]`, then 32 bits at `+0xc`. Order from `PlayerAction::set` `0x081128a0` (channel 0..5 → action channel 0..5, mask bit i gates input channel i) and the `c_PIYaw … c_PIMouseLookY` rodata at `0x086c86a5`. [netcode.md §1](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| W-2 | Encode is **`floatToFixed(v, 12, 16.0)`**: `v/16` clamped to ±1, then 12-bit — `round(4095·(clamp(v/16, −1, 1) + 1)/2)` | **verified** | `floatToFixed` `0x08113480` (`fdivrp` by 16.0, `fucom` clamps via `test ah,0x45`, `(1<<bits)−1 · (v/16+1) · 0.5`); constants as bytes: −1.0f `0x86b05ec`, 0.5 `0x86be450`, 16.0f `0x41800000` at every `set` channel. The same ±16 headroom GUN-2b warns about. [netcode.md §1](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| W-3 | Decode is **`((2n/4095) − 1)·16` rounded to 0.01**, gated per channel on `isInputMapped` | **verified** | `PlayerAction::get` `0x0815c5a0`: `n/4095.0` (`0x86c0cf0`), ×2, −1, ×16.0 (`0x86c0cf8`), ×100 (`0x86b01ac`), `frndint`, ÷100. [netcode.md §1](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| W-4 | **ch48 (Communication) never crosses the wire** — receivers reconstruct it as 0 from an initialized-zero `PlayerAction` | **verified** | `get` reads ch48 from byte 16 (`param_1+8` dword bit 0x100); `writeToStream`/`readFromStream` only cover bytes 0–15. [netcode.md §1](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| W-5 | **`operator<<`/`>>` (`0x081d89f0`/`0x081d8bc0`) are console name-index helpers, NOT the wire format** — the design README's "`>>` operator is the spec for our wire format" is overturned | **verified** (corrects [`features/netcode-play-multiplayer/README.md`](../netcode-play-multiplayer/README.md)) | `operator<<` is a 56-case `switch` over channel names in rodata (`0x086c86a5..`, jump table `0x086c88c4`), no serialization of any kind; `operator>>` reads a `std::string` and `compare`s it against the same names. The wire spec is `PlayerAction` (W-1…W-3). [netcode.md §1](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-1 | The server's per-frame order is **processLocalPlayersInputs → processReceivedPackets → one simulateFrame per tick → updateStateMasks/updateVoteSystem/updateBanList → updateConnections → processGameStateAndSendPackets (the send) → clearPlayerActions** | **verified** | `GameServer::update` `0x08132940`; the per-tick call is GameServer vtable `0x0871b0e0`+8 slot **+0x140** = `simulateFrame` `0x0815c2a0`. [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-2 | Each remote player's buffered actions are trimmed to **≤ 4, dropping the OLDEST**; a trim also writes `player+0x174 = 0` | **verified** | `clearPlayerActions` `0x0815bb90`: per connection, player's ActionBuffer at `BFPlayer+0x144`; `while (4 < size)` pop the oldest. [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-3 | **Exactly one** action is consumed per player per tick | **verified** | `GameServer::simulatePlayerUpdate` `0x0815bd00` reads the FIRST ActionBuffer entry and erases it; `simulatePlayersUpdate` `0x0815bfa0` calls it once per player per tick (remote connections); sets `player+0x14b`. [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-4 | Empty buffer → **zeroed input**; the receive side **dedupes by serial** (drops `≤ last`); a backlog **> 9 pending ticks collapses to 1** (both binaries) | **verified** (all three) | Empty: `0x0815bd00` decodes the sentinel and runs an explicit 55-float zero loop. Dedupe: `processRcvdPlayerActions` `0x08148470` pushBack only when the entry's serial (`+0x88`) is `> lastSerial` (`+0x68`); serial signed 32-bit, count field 4-bit in `processReceivedPacket` `0x081484e0`. Backlog: server `0x08132940` (`9 < n → n = 1`); client `GameClient::update` `0x0048fca0` `cmp ebx,0x9; jle` at `0x48fca8`–`0x48fcb1` (bridge had lost this function — re-derived from PE bytes). [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-5 | Client routing is `Setup::dispatchPlayerInput` → `Game::addPlayerInput`, a **`map<int, list<PlayerInput>>` insert** per tick | **verified** | `0x00448520` ends in `Game__addPlayerInput` `0x0040ecb0` (PE bytes): map find/insert on `Game+0x2c` (`0x0063c8a0`), `list::push_back` (`0x0040e270`), `ret 0x8`. [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-6 | Client consume: **one ActionBuffer pop per local player per tick** — pack via `PlayerAction::set`, then get → `handleInput` → `performHandleUpdate` | **verified** | `GameClient::processLocalPlayersInputs` `0x00488840` (live); `GameClient::simulatePlayerUpdate` `0x004b6a30` (live): pops one `PlayerAction::get` (`0x004913a0`) entry, erase, `handleInput` vtable+0x30, `performHandleUpdate` `0x004b7680`. [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| J-1 | The server join handshake is **`receive()==1` → new ClientConnection → ServerInfoEvent/ServerInfoEvent2 → ChallengeEvent → chokeBandwidth**; the ctor fixes **20 Hz send rate (conn+0xc), 1044-byte budget (conn+0x10), 1024-byte send `BitStream(0x400)`** | **verified** | `processReceivedPackets` `0x08137ba0`; ClientConnection ctor `0x081142a0` (`param_1[3]` = raw bits `0x14` = 20, `param_1[4]` = raw bits `0x414` = 1044). The second BitStream ctor there is invoked with no explicit arg — its default limit stays **open**. [netcode.md §3](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| J-2 | **IService / IJoinService / IHostService are empty stubs with no handshake** — every vtable slot is the same single import stub; the design README's "joiners connect to a dedicated host service" must not be built on them | **verified** (corrects [`features/netcode-play-multiplayer/README.md`](../netcode-play-multiplayer/README.md)) | Client PE: `0x4010b0`/`0x4010e0`/`0x401120` load vtables `0x8c37d8`/`0x8c37e0`/`0x8c3810` (plus adjacent copies `0x4010c0`/`0x4010f0`/`0x401130`/`0x401140`); all three vtables' slots = `0x00804ae0` = `jmp ds:0x8c352c`. The real handshake is J-1's server flow. [netcode.md §3](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| J-3 | Disconnect is an **explicit packet type 0xd, then teardown** | **verified** | `GameClient::disconnect` `0x00490f00` (PE bytes, full body): `push 0xd; call [vtable+0x18]` at `0x490f47`, then `+0x88` cleared, state `+0x8`/`+0xc` set to 4, `+0x34` nulled, held objects released. [netcode.md §3](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| J-4 | The bf42plus netcode patches are failure-mode history for the above | **verified** | `0x00490f4c` (inside disconnect; `GameClient_disconnect_udp` force-disconnect point), `0x00495fd2` (`force_disconnect_msg` via `0x6a7ee0`), `0x006d8461` (`skip_spawn_screen_join`: `SpawnScreenStuff::setVisible(1)` via `0x6cce20`), `0x004b4045` (`network_error_debug` / GameEvent deserialize site). [netcode.md §3](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| P-1 | The client predicts its own player each tick **from the same `PlayerAction` it registers for the wire**; `registerPlayerAction` `0x004904f0` is a small cache-and-call stub, not a re-pack | **verified** (loop level); the exact register→wire send path stays **open** (NEEDS-MORE, cosmetic) | Per-player loop `0x004b8d70` (live): front pending input → set `0x00483e70` → get `0x004913a0` → `handleInput` vtable+0x30 → `performHandleUpdate` `0x004b7680` → `0x004904f0` → pop. `0x004904f0`'s live body: cache `FUN_00484820(p)+0x14` into `param_1+0x1c8`, call `FUN_004b7200`. [netcode.md §4](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| P-2 | Remote players are stepped by the client's own sim **at zero input**, then overwritten by server ghost state every **0.1 s**; **there is no interpolation buffer** | **verified** (the no-buffer claim is read-backed 2026-09-20); the apply step `0x00498ce0` stays **open** | `GameClient::simulatePlayersUpdate` `0x004b6c20` calls `0x004b6a30` (which zeroes input on an empty buffer) per non-local player. Ghost: server `GhostManager::update` `0x08141c70`; client twin `0x0048a130` (read live — UNREAD at research time; gated on `GameClient+0x1d8`) → state-applier `0x00489ed0` → per-entity distance/velocity factor `0x00489cb0` → **`0x00498ce0` unread**. The path holds no snapshot-history/sample repository — single current state with a computed factor. Cadence: `conn+0x18 = 0x3dcccccd` at server `0x08139420` and client `0x0048c520` (0.1f at `0x8c53cc`). [netcode.md §4](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| R-1 | Default per-connection send rate **20 Hz, minimum 10**; the choke lowers by 5 to the floor of 10 while **Σ(rate × 1044) > cap** (`GameServer+0x46c`) | **verified** | `chokeBandwidth` `0x08132cc0` / `setBandwidthChokeLimit` `0x08132c90` / `getBandwidthChokeLimit` `0x08132cb0`; the rate is applied as period `1/rate` in `processGameStateAndSendPackets` `0x08139420` (client twin `0x0048c520`) and written via `changeUpdateFrequency` `0x08132ff0` → `conn+0xc`. [netcode.md §5](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| R-2 | The packet carries a **4-bit action count (≤ 15)**; `GameServer::receive` is the receive path; `setPacketSize` is an **empty stub** | **verified** | `processReceivedPacket` `0x081484e0` reads `readUnsigned(stream, 4)`, each counted entry `readFromStream`. `GameServer::receive` `0x081378d0` (NetServer read into the per-connection BitStream at `conn+0x90`, `setMode(read)`). `setPacketSize` `0x08132c80` = `return;`. [netcode.md §5](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| R-2b | "The client batches ≤ 3 actions per packet" | **open** — the ≤ 3 cap exists only **server-side** in `PlayerActionManager::add` `0x08148120` (`if (3 < count) count = 3`, ring memmove), and that class is reachable only indirectly (one pointer hit → a vtable/interface slot) and server-managed; the client PE has no `PlayerActionManager` and the client send loop was not re-derivable. Do not record "≤ 3" as client-verified wire behaviour | R-2 verdict (NEEDS-MORE). [netcode.md §5–§6](subsystems/netcode.md) |

## Sound runtime: listener, attached parts, distance law (2026-09-25)

Write-up: [`features/sound-listener-parity/README.md`](../sound-listener-parity/README.md).

| # | Assumption in our code | Where | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| SND-1 | `setAttachToListener` is a bool on the sound half of every object template | `bf42/con.py` `attach_to_listener`, `extract_map.py` `attachToListener` | **verified** | `SimpleSoundTemplate::setAttachToListener` lnxded `0x081de9c0` writes byte `+4`, getter `0x081de9d0`; console `ConsoleClass144::executeObjectMethod` `0x081c9bf0` (template `+0x28`); `makeScript` emits `ObjectTemplate.setAttachToListener 1` |
| SND-2 | A part is attached only while the local player sits Inside the PlayerControlObject it belongs to | `vehicle-audio.js` `_attached` | **verified** | `SimpleObject::attachToListener` lnxded `0x081db610`, client `0x00536390`: flag, `getLocalHumanPlayer` (`PlayerManager+0x20`), `BFPlayer::getVehicle` (`+0x3c`), nearest `IID_IPlayerControlObject` (`0xc4c5`) up the `+0x50` parent chain equal for both (client `0x00406840`), `getCamera()->getViewMode()` is 3 or 0 (CAM-1) |
| SND-3 | Engine, RotationalBundle and FireArms apply it every frame | `vehicle-audio.js` `update` | **verified** | lnxded `Engine::updateSound` `0x0823e930`, `RotationalBundle::updateSound` `0x081d8630`, `FireArms::updateSound` `0x0828cc10`; client `0x0057e924`, `0x0057cd1e`, `0x0053a0fe` -> sound vtable `0x008fd31c` slot 0x24 -> patch `0x00947948` slot 0x30 -> sample `0x00947bb8` slot 0x2c `0x008020e0` (byte `+0xc0`) |
| SND-4 | An attached voice sits at the listener; `relativePosition` ignored; `Distance` stays the true distance | `engine-audio.js` `setAttachedToListener` | **verified** (the viewer's dead-ahead panner is its rendering of "at the listener") | client `0x00802330`: `+0xc0` -> `listener->getPosition()` (`0x0080237a`); else `0x00802620` (owner transform x `relativePosition`); `Distance` control `0x00802950` -> `0x008028c0`, from `0x00802620` |
| SND-5 | The listener is the camera | `page-audio.js` `updateAudio` | **verified** | client `0x00537390`: the local player's camera `getTransformation` -> DS3D listener position and orientation; velocity from the camera root's physics, 0 in fly-by |
| SND-6 | Spatialised voices fall off as `minDistance / d`, with no maximum | `engine-audio.js` `distanceRolloff` | **verified**, except which layer word feeds `SetMinDistance`: **inferred** `minDistance` | voice init `SetMinDistance(1.0)` `0x007fefee`, `SetMaxDistance(1e9)` `0x007feffa`; per frame `0x00802444` from layer `+0x24`; listener rolloff 1.0 (`0x00663eb0`); DS3D flush `0x00837f10` (codes 6/7 -> `SetMaxDistance` / `SetMinDistance`) |
| SND-7 | Every land-vehicle Engine carries the flag; no aircraft or ship Engine, and no FireArms, does | `ssc-specs.js` `attachesToListener` fallback | **verified** over vanilla / XPack1 / XPack2 `Objects.rfa` | 65 / 9 / 31 occurrences; land Engines 19 / 4 / 22 all flagged (sole exception `katyusharocket_engine`, a projectile); air 16 / 4 / 25 and sea 14 / 1 / 1 none; FireArms none |
